  - **The tofu and soba shop** (`g4`, `construireBoutique`; outside the そば noren, the 五箇山 とうふ・そば sign under the eave, a red 手打ちそば banner, a bench): earth floor; the counter and its souvenirs (Gokayama's firm tofu tied with straw rope, boxes of sweets, washi cards, a maneki-neko), closed at both ends by low gates; the seller (`vendeuse`: a taste of tofu, `tofu` + companion; closed at night), shelves (sake bottles, pots, towels); the kitchen (the clay stove and its glowing mouths, the pot that steams, soba boxes, the cutting table) and the cook (`cuisinier`); the table and benches, two bowls of soba, a tourist eating (`touriste`, seated); a tatami platform with a low table and a grandpa drinking tea (`grandpere`); the menu on wooden boards (« Lire »), two paper lanterns.
  - **Around**: the minshuku's front (indigo 民宿 noren, lantern, the 民宿 庄川荘 sign, a veranda one steps onto and sits on (since the third round), a woodpile); paddies (`RIZIERES`, some just planted; the rice, `sorte` 25 `riz`, is harvested in winter: it falls like the crowns) one now wades into (`flaques`: the water's height there; their walls removed; rings at each step); the Jinushi shrine on an esplanade cut into the mountain (stone wall and steps, rebuilt in the third round, see below; grey torii, komainu, the haiden with bell and offering box, festival lanterns, cedars; the esplanade is a surface: its steep edge, natural ground, blocked the top of the steps); 8 water-cannon huts (`POMPES`); the grandmother's washi workshop (the vat an open box of boards, she and the traveller beside it, the panel out of the drying boards); gardens, fences; the path's lanterns at their own ground height (`lanterne(…, y0)` in `modeles3d.js`; some stood half in the ground). The welcome sign stands by the path in front of the start.
  - **The fire brigade's shed** (消防団, `CASERNE`; it was an unreadable box): plank walls, a grey shutter, a tin roof, the red lamp, buckets, a hose reel, a hydrant; beside it the rust-red fire lookout tower (火の見櫓) and its bell.
  - **The stream** (`RUISSEAU`, a new course from the forest to the gorge; `RU` samples it every 25 cm: its stepped water level, foam where it drops; `versRuisseau`): one continuous wall geometry on each side, mossy rocks on its drops, stones in its bed; the water is a ribbon with a flowing shader (`courants`, `creerEauCourante` in `balade.js`: streaks going downstream, ripples, foam, lamplight at night), and the cascade into the gorge. The ground under it, its walls and the stairs uses `solDetaille` (4 × 4 subdivided ground cells: the 1 m mesh cut the stream). **The bridges** (`PONTS`: where the main path crosses, and since the third round a second one where `CHEMIN_PONT`, from house g2's path to the torii, crosses; the stream's walls stopped crossing them): beams, a plank deck, curbs, posts and bamboo rails, end stones.
  - **The belvedere stairs** (`ESC`, replacing the switchback path, « pas pratique du tout »): one straight flight from the village edge to the platform, its profile smoothed from the ground and never going down, equal 19 cm risers; solid stone steps, stepped side walls, handrails, two lanterns at the foot (alternate step and wall widths differ by 1 cm: their 4 cm overlaps were coplanar). `BELVEDERE` (−58.5, −64.5): the postcard view over the village.
  - **The arrival** (« là où il y a le bus, faudrait le refaire… et quand on sort… un mur invisible »): the world-heritage bus (世界遺産バス, 相倉口 ⇄ 高岡駅), the shelter (待合所: windows, posts, the stop's name board, a village poster drawn by `affiche()`, timetable, a bench), the bus-stop pole, the parking lines; the road climbs north to a tunnel (`TUN`, `TUBE`, `FACADE`, `versTunnel`/`duTunnel`; 相倉トンネル plate, lamps, the tube darkening; `trouSol` leaves out the ground cells inside it) between cut slopes with a concrete frame in relief (法枠: 32 cm beams following the slope, 2 cm higher one way than the other), white edge lines, delineators, a barrier with a no-pedestrians sign (`pietonInterdit()`) where the walk ends, a blue direction sign. `dansLeCadre`: the river-side band now joins the terrace edge smoothly (`lisse(32, 42, riv.d)`): the old cut left a notch of invisible wall north of the parking.
  - **The village edge** (« les arbres… qui lévitent sur une espèce de texture verte »): wherever the walk stops on the mountain side (`dansLeCadre` scanned on a 1 m grid, cells within 3.2 m of a walkable one, not the gorge), an undergrowth hem: kumazasa clumps (a dark dome and fans of long leaves, `sorte` `conifere`: evergreen, snow on top), ferns, shrubs, young cedars; so the edge shows instead of an invisible wall in the grass. The forest is denser within 30 m of it (cedars mostly) and on the far bank of the river (cedars, evergreen oaks with trunks, deciduous trees), trunks start under the lowest ground around them (cedars with a flared foot), every tree is noted in `ARBRES_SOL` and `aspectSol` darkens the forest floor (olive leaf litter and moss) under it.
  - **Stamps**: `tenbo` (the belvedere's view), `jinja` (pray: bell, two claps, gesture `prier`), `washi` (dip the screen with the grandmother: gesture `washi`), `kokiriko` (dance with the dancer: the traveller is put on the stage beside him, `jeu.placer(x, z, cap, y)`, and seen from the audience between the two lanterns; gesture `kokiriko`; the musicians' loop `kokiriko` is heard as the Noh is, `musiciens.son`, and the walk's music dips), `hosui` (the firefighter's drill: the 8 hut doors open, the cannons rise and tilt and throw arcs of droplets with a rainbow, `creerEssai`, seen from a high camera).
  - **Reward: the mino** (蓑, the straw rain cape): a new wardrobe slot `dos` (on the back: the everyday backpack `sac`, default, or the mino, which hides it; `capeMino` in `personnages.js`, also worn by the farmer).
  - **Secret** (`SECRETS.gokayama = 'heike'`, « Les lucioles des Heike », since 2026-10-24: the owner found the first one, three hollow-sounding boards of the museum floor over a saltpetre pit with a Heike banner, « pas assez bien » and picked this one among four proposals). The legend: after Dan-no-ura (1185) the last Heike hid in these mountains disguised as farmers, and their souls became fireflies (the small river fireflies are still called Heike-botaru). The hints: the shrine keeper and the schoolboy (a golden firefly by the stream at night; follow it). At night (`c.nuit`), among the stream's fireflies, a golden one waits at the first point of `LUCIOLE` (on the north bank by the minshuku, 10 m from the okami who gives the night, since the third round); the first time the player comes within 12 m the camera shows it over the traveller's shoulder (`lucioleLoin`); within 3.6 m it says so (`lucioleDoree`, the companion's line) and flies to the next point (`heike` in `construire`: hover, then a 2.6 m/s eased hop with a little arc and a trail of sparks), waiting there for the player each time: along the north bank to the second bridge, in front of the torii, up the shrine stairs, north of the shrine, to `TOMBE` under the cedars behind the shrine, a mossy gorintō (five stones, 平 carved on the side facing the shrine, an offering stone, bamboo vases, ferns, moss on the ground: `aspectSol`). There the camera frames the grave from the side (`tombe`), the firefly settles on top, 36 fireflies light up and rise toward the cedars (`essaim`), the koban appears on the offering stone (`tombeLueur`, the companion's line); « Prendre » it (`legende`) gives the koban. By day the firefly is gone and the grave says `tombeJour`; at dusk it waits again by the stream; once found, it watches over the grave at night with a few fireflies around, and the grave can be prayed at (`tombeApres`). It is a `THREE.Points` glow (its size corrected for the camera's field of view, `contexteAnime.champ`: three.js point sizes ignore it, and on a tall screen they looked huge), a spark of constant screen size (`phare`, `sizeAttenuation: false`: seen from far away) and a trail (`trainee`), carrying a small moving lamp (`lueurs.js`: a light with `mobile: true` has its position read every frame). `gk_heike.py [--tel] [--compagnon c]` gives the night, stands at the okami and follows it to the koban (31–33 s of walking).
  - **Bunbuku** under the cedars below the shrine (`MARCHAND` −52, −37); **fireworks** over the gorge to the east (`feux` x 95).
  - **Night**: the minshuku's okami offers the night (and the morning back). Lit shōji and lanterns, fireflies along the stream, frogs croaking from the paddies (loop `grenouilles`, by distance to `mares`, at night only), night lines; the shrine keeper, the grandmother and the others say other things.
  - **Animals** (`betes.js`, `formesGokayama`): grey herons fishing in the paddies, flying slowly to another one when one comes near; tree frogs on the dikes, jumping into the water; red dragonflies (akatombo) darting, hovering, landing; the minshuku's shiba at its door (barks, `ouaf`, when one arrives; pet it; lies down at night); fireflies at night.
  - **Passers-by**: the okami, the washi grandmother, the kokiriko dancer (pose `kokiriko`, the sasara) and musician (the kokiriko sticks), the firefighter, a farmer (sugegasa and mino), the museum guide, a schoolboy, a photographer at the belvedere, the shrine keeper (pose `balai`), and @random_japan_place filming by the river (the owner's TikTok).
  - **Sound and music**: loops `riviere`, `ruisseau` (by distance to `ruisseaux`), `grenouilles`, `kokiriko`; sounds `aosagi` (the heron), `ouaf`, `creux`, `jets`, `papier`, `cloche`, `claque`; birds uguisu, black kite, heron. Its tune (`AIRS.gokayama`): A minor on the min'yō scale (la do ré mi sol), shakuhachi, shamisen arpeggios, the kokiriko's beat (taiko, sasara on 2 and 4, bamboo sticks off-beat), 96 BPM; at night the music box at 72.
  - **Drawing it from above.** From the belvedere one sees the whole village: 325 draw calls instead of about 180, and 16.6 ms frames at CPU ÷4 in a phone context (the village: 5.6). A CDP profile showed the cost was per object (three.js's `renderBufferDirect`/`setProgram`, `updateMatrixWorld`), and that each passer-by is 12 draw calls (6 parts × body and ink outline). Now (`balade.js`, for every place): beyond `TRAIT_PASSANT` (60 m) a passer-by has no outline (1–2 px there), and top-level objects that are hidden (the companions not chosen, the other traveller, far animals) skip their matrix updates (`matrixWorldAutoUpdate = visible` before each render). Belvedere: 5.6 ms (p95 11), like the village; Itsukushima and Aogashima unchanged.
  - Checks (the 2026-10-23 session's scratchpad `gokayama/`): `gk_test.mjs` (builds the place in Node, walks from the start with the game's rules: every interaction reachable), `plan2.mjs` + `plan2.py` (a plan of heights, reach, walls, interactions), `gk_vues.py [prefix] [--nuit] [--saison s] [--tel]` (set views), `gk_partie.py [--tel] [--compagnon c] [--garcon]` (the 5 stamps, the mino, the secret, night and morning), `gk_secret.py`, `gk_jets.py`, `gk_tenue.py`, `gk_fluide.py <place>` (frame times at set views, CPU ÷4), `gk_profil.py` (CDP profile, readable three.js routed in), `gk_appels.py <place>` (draw calls per view), `gk_dessines.py` (what is drawn, by scene group); and in `balade/`: `choix_test.py <dir> gokayama [--premiere]`, `coplanaires_gros.mjs gokayama` (run with `node --max-old-space-size=14000`: the plain one ran out of memory on this place's 428k triangles and its huge background quads; big triangles are tested against their whole plane). Coplanar faces left: hidden ones (wall tops under the thatch, faces inside the crowns) or of the same colour. Since 2026-10-24 (687k triangles, the same frame times): `gk_test.mjs` also walks up the ladder (by floor), `gk_marche.py <routes>` (holding W along routes: the parking exits, the shrine, the belvedere), `gk_grenier.py` (climb, walk, the racks, the trapdoor's edge, climb down), `gk_dedans.py` (walk into the minshuku and the shop, every person and object there, the nap and the night), `sonde.mjs x z` (what the world says around a point), `bord.mjs` (the frame's edge by angle), and in `balade/` `copl_bornes.mjs` / `copl_mm.mjs` (one object's overlaps inside bounds; with planes bucketed by the millimetre: the centimetre buckets reported faces 4–5 mm apart, which do not flicker).
- **What the walk's engine gained with Gokayama (2026-10-24).** `lieu.solDetaille(x0, z0, x1, z1)`: ground cells drawn 4 × 4 (their edges stay linear where they meet ordinary cells). `lieu.courants`: flowing water (`creerEauCourante`: `uv.x` metres downstream, `uv.y` across, an `ecume` attribute). `lieu.flaques(x, z)`: the water height in shallow places (`niveauEau`: splashes, rings, wet clothes). `jeu.grimper(points, duree, cap)` and the gesture `grimper` (`personnages.js`); the companion joins the player when they are on different floors. `monde.js`: a surface with `etage` is ignored by `hauteur(x, z)` without `y` (and `passage` lets one walk under it). Passers-by with `dedans` hide when the camera is far outside their house. Verbs `monter`, `descendre`, `dormir`, `gouter` (`textes.js`). `lanterne(x, z, s, y0)` (`modeles3d.js`). `poserPied` (`monde.js`): a surface within 2 cm under the natural ground wins over it (since the third round; it had to be level).
- **Graphics pass (2026-10-24).** The owner installed 3D skills (shader-techniques, texturing, game-developer…) and asked to « améliorer les graphismes » of the three places. Before/after views (`beaute.py` in the session scratchpad: set views per place, `--nuit`, `--tel`, `--port 8124` for the committed copy) showed the water (flat, with blinking 25 cm squares), the flat light and the night as the weak points. Every place got:
  - **Water** (`EAU_GLSL` in `styles.js`, used by the sea or river `creerEau`, the stream `creerEauCourante` and the still water `creerEauxCalmes`): ripples from a value noise with exact derivatives (three octaves, one read each) that fade beyond 18–120 m (else they shimmer), the sky reflected by a Schlick Fresnel (the sky's own colour uniforms, so night follows; strength 0.42 on the sea, which otherwise turned pale white to the horizon), crisp sun glints and a sun path (the moon's at night). The sea also has the wave that runs up the beach and back (a foam line moving through the depth), and foam collars and rings around what stands in the water: `carteContactEau` cuts every decor triangle crossing the water level on the fine extent (quarter metre), then a chamfer distance transform (2 m → 255).
  - **Caustics** on the bottom under shallow water (`CAUSTIQUES_GLSL` in the ground shader): the edges of two animated Voronoi patterns (F2 − F1), under `eauSol` = (water level, on, time, night), faded with depth (2–260 cm), by `fwidth` far away and at night.
  - **Ground occlusion** (`carteOmbreSol`, `OMBRE_SOL_GLSL`): near-vertical decor faces 0–2.2 m above the ground (walls, trunks, posts, rocks, fences, bushes), sampled one point per 0.25 m² and weighted by height, blurred (three 5-cell cross blurs at 0.5 m), darken the ground at their foot (to 0.5).
  - **Light on things** (`REFLETS_DECOR`, after the lights): a crisp highlight on lacquer, tin and a little on tiles; foliage seen against the sun glows golden-green. Characters get a soft rim on the sun's side (`LISERE_PERSO`, bluish under the moon).
  - **Glow** (`passeFinale`): a quarter-size bright pass (soft knee, a Karis average of its four taps since the third round) blurred horizontally and vertically (9-tap Gaussian in 5 reads) and added; threshold 0.97 and strength 0.25 by day (glints, the sun, sunlit shōji inside), 0.55 and 0.82 at night (lanterns, windows; it was 0.5 and 0.9), blurred a second time, 2.2× wider, at night; set by `finale.nuit(v)`.
  - **Light shafts** (`lieu.rais`, `creerRais`: open cones leaning along `lieu.soleil`, additive, striped and slowly moving, faded near the eye, far away and at night): under the cedars of Gokayama's shrine and around Bunbuku in all three places.
  - **Gokayama**: valley mist (`lieu.brumeVallee` { bas, haut, force, debut }: in the final pass, each pixel's world position from the depth, fog colour, thicker low down and far away; 0.4× at night so the lit windows show); the paddies are real still water (`lieu.eauxCalmes`: mud green, the sky mirrored at grazing angles, the banks' dark reflection along the edges) over a mud box 3 cm lower (no z-fighting far away). **Aogashima**: Maruyama's ravines have uneven widths and wander (they were equal stripes, « like a melon »).
  - Cost (`gpu_charge.py`: the game loop stopped, 60 frames drawn at 3200 × 1800 with 4× MSAA, then one pixel read back; before and after alternated): Itsukushima 1.2–1.4 → 1.3–1.5 ms, Aogashima unchanged, Gokayama 2.2–2.5 → 2.5–2.8 ms; the phone walk measure (`fluidite_port.py`) stays at the same frame rates (its numbers swing between runs with the GPU's clock). Checks: `gk_test.mjs`, `gk_partie.py`, `gk_dedans.py`, `partie.py`, `nuit.py`.
- **Trees and houses (2026-10-24, the owner: « améliore aussi les modèles 3D des maisons et des arbres mais fais toujours attention que les balades… restent fluides sur tous les supports »).** Close views (`modeles.py` in the session scratchpad, `--saison`, `--tel`, `--port 8124`) showed faceted balls on sticks for broadleaf trees, three stacked cones for cedars, square-ish trunks at Aogashima and flat thatch slabs. New builders in the atelier of `modeles3d.js`, used by the walk only (the map's models keep `arbre`, `cedre`, `pinJapon`, `buisson` unchanged):
  - `houppe(r, c, o, finesse)`: a foliage clump, an icosahedron bumped by smooth lobes (always the same at the same place) and shaded by its height inside the clump (`fin()`'s new per-piece `volume` [x, y, z, r]: dark underside, lit top); `buisson(…, { touffu: true })` uses it.
  - `fut(de, vers, rBas, rHaut, c, cotes)`: a tapering trunk or branch, open-ended (its ends are in the ground or the foliage: half a `baton`'s triangles).
  - `arbreTouffu(x, z, h, couleurs, o)`: a leaning tapered trunk with a root flare, three main branches, eight clumps (top, a ring of five, two small low ones at finesse 0); `caduc`: a light winter frame (a branch and three twigs under the four big clumps); `loin`: four clumps, no flare.
  - `sugi(x, z, h, o)`: a tall straight red-brown trunk with a flared foot, five tiers of jagged cones (alternating point radii, drooping tips, hollow undersides, darker below), smaller upward; `loin`: three tiers.
  - `pinNuages(x, z, s, sens)`: a cloud-pruned pine (niwaki): a waving three-segment trunk with ball joints, arms to seven flattened, darker-below needle pads.
  - **Where**: Gokayama's village trees and, within 40 m of the walkable area (`pres`), the forest's cedars (sugi) and broadleaves; the hem's young cedars are sugi and its shrubs and the hedges `houppe`; Itsukushima's ten pines and the Noh stage's three (`pinNuages`), its six maples (`arbreTouffu`, red ones `houppierErable`); Aogashima's crater and merchant-grove trees (`arbreTouffu`, keeping their three `tire()` colour draws so the rest of the random decor does not move), its wall and flank forests and bushes (`houppe`: same triangle count as the balls).
  - **Houses**: the gasshō thatch slabs are subdivided (10 × ~L/0.85 cells) and bumped (`bosses`: wide soft waves and a slight crown on top, the eave 22 cm thicker and cut clean); the new/old thatch patches follow the slab's grid cells (on smaller triangles, world-space patches made staircases). Under the eaves, a grey pebble drip line in the ground map (`aspectSol`, amadare). Itsukushima's shop houses got dark posts and a beam on their white side walls and an upper-floor mushiko-mado (white plaster, eleven slats, two rails).
  - **Paying for it** (asset-optimization: spatial partitioning and outline LOD). Gokayama's forest and hem were three meshes of 230 000, 100 000 and 26 000 triangles plus a 113 000-triangle hem, always drawn whole, even behind the camera. They are now cut into pieces (`foret(x, z)`: eight sectors around the village, and beyond 130 m four more), so the camera culls what it does not see; the far pieces have no ink outline (`poser(…, { trait: false })` → `maillage(geo, ombre, trait)` in `balade.js`: about one pixel there, softened by the distance blur anyway). Triangles drawn per frame (`appels.py`, phone 390 × 844 ×2): Gokayama centre 1.79 M → 1.26 M, belvedere 1.84 → 1.49, edge 1.64 → 1.12, start 1.81 → 1.50; Aogashima +4–8 %; Itsukushima +1 %. Draw calls +0 to +11 (the pieces). GPU (`gpu_charge.py`, against this morning's version, before both passes): Gokayama 2.1–2.4 → 2.1 ms, Itsukushima and Aogashima +0–0.15 ms. Phone CPU ÷4 (`gk_fluide.py`): unchanged medians (5.6 ms) everywhere, the belvedere 5.6–11 (it swings between runs). Checks: `gk_test.mjs` (55 594 cells, nothing out of reach), `regions.mjs` against this morning's copy (same reachable cells), `coplanaires_gros.mjs gokayama` (no new overlap; the forest has fewer), `copl_mm.mjs itsukushima 51` (the shop: only hidden underground faces), `gk_partie.py`, `gk_grenier.py`, `partie.py`, `nuit.py`, `marchand_vues.py`.
- **Characters, animals, tents and the pagoda (2026-10-24, « améliore aussi les personnages et les animaux de la même façon, les tentes, la pagode »).** Close views (`persos.py`, and `portraits.py` with the game's free camera `jeu.cadrer`, in the session scratchpad; `--port 8124` for this morning's copy):
  - **Passers-by** (`creerPersonnage`) get the travellers' face, drawn on the head mesh itself (`surVisage`; a point on the eye's plane turns with it by Y then X, like the Euler angles): a narrowing jaw, eyes as flat discs (white, iris `T.iris` or brown, pupil, two highlights, lash line), or a happy arc for `yeux: 'plisses'`, eyebrows in the hair colour (grey for bald or white-haired), a nose, a smile, cheeks; five fringe locks and two side locks; a flatter torso with a waist, a neck; an obi (and its knot) on kimonos not under a hakama (`T.obi`, else `T.col`); rounded hanging kimono sleeves; hands with a thumb. Their eyes do not blink: separate eye meshes would add draw calls for every passer-by.
  - **Deer** (`creerDaim`): a sculpted body (deep chest, tucked belly, round rump), the dark dorsal stripe, rows of white summer spots, the white rump patch ringed in black and the tail; legs in two parts (fleshy top, thin darker shank, hind hocks set back) with black hooves; a tapering neck, a skull and a narrowing muzzle, black nose and mouth, eyes with a highlight, large dark-rimmed ears; the stag's antlers have three points.
  - **Animals**: gulls and crows have a teardrop body and an eye highlight (the gull: the black band on its bill, white mirrors on its wing tips); cats have whiskers, a pink nose and a Y mouth; the shiba eye highlights, its cream « maro » eyebrows and a mouth; the heron a teardrop body, hanging breast plumes, two nape plumes and a yellow eye. The small ones (thrush, skink, frogs, dragonflies, butterflies) are too small on screen to gain anything.
  - **Tents** (Aogashima's camping): the dome is a two-pole igloo tent (a rounded-square base, the darker pole sleeves crossing on top, the bathtub floor's dark band — the whole last ring of triangles —, a D-shaped door that follows the fabric: `surDome` finds the point on the deformed dome for (side, height) by bisection, in rings so no chord passes under the curve; the rolled door fabric, guy lines and pegs); the ridge tent has a slightly sagging ridge, faces turned outward by `dehors`, two poles, an open door with its mesh and rolled flaps, guy lines.
  - **The pagoda** (Itsukushima's five-storey gojū-no-tō): per storey, posts dividing each face in three bays, a plank door in the middle, white walls and slatted windows (renji-mado) on the sides, beams; under each roof, three tiers of brackets (kumimono) rising to the roof's underside (`pointToit` repeats `toitJapon`'s formula), and fifteen rafters per side following that underside, white-tipped; balconies with railings on the upper storeys; a bronze wind bell at each roof corner; the spire with its base, inverted bowl, lotus, nine rings, flame plates and jewel. Pieces of two neighbouring faces cross at the corners: every other face is offset by 12 mm (`q`) so none share a plane (`copl_mm.mjs itsukushima 57`: only post bottoms inside the balcony floor remain).
  - **Cost**: per-object draw calls unchanged (the faces are part of the head mesh). Triangles per frame (`appels.py`, phone): Itsukushima start 443 k → 486 k, pagoda view 377 k → 470 k (still the lightest place: Aogashima ≈ 0.8 M, Gokayama 1.1–1.5 M); Aogashima +5–8 %; Gokayama still 16–31 % under this morning's. Phone CPU ÷4 (`gk_fluide.py`, alternating): no regression (the machine was busier during this run, both versions slower alike). Checks: `partie.py`, `nuit.py`, `nuit2_test.py itsukushima`, `gk_partie.py`, `gk_dedans.py`, `coplanaires.mjs` (pagoda and tents).
- **Gokayama, the owner's next round (2026-10-24, evening; ten screenshots).**
  - **The stream's water is the paddies' water** (« la même eau que dans les rizières »; it was a pale turquoise with white streaks and a foam line along both walls). `creerEauCourante` (`balade.js`) now follows `creerEauxCalmes`: mud green, the sky mirrored at grazing angles (0.85), the stone walls' dark reflection along the edges, ripples computed in the bed's own frame and turned into the world by the new `sens` attribute (the direction of the current), so they flow downstream; white only where it falls (`ecume`); double-sided (the cascade is seen from behind and above).
  - **The cascade.** The bed stopped 3 m short of the terrace edge and the water ran flat over the grass, grass tufts through it. `RUISSEAU` now ends at the edge (66.85, −19.24); `CASCADE` (module level) arcs out of the bed (a free fall, never higher than the bed's end) and runs down the bank to the river; `aspectSol` paints wet rock under it (`CASCADE_PLAN`, `CASCADE_BORNES`: no tufts), mossy rocks on both sides.
  - **Its sound** (« vraiment horrible »): `boucle/ruisseau` was 170 long singing bubbles a second (10–32 ms, +70 % glide: a boiling pot) over a flat hiss up to 8 kHz. Now (`bruitage-calcul.js`): two bands of water noise whose strength trembles (`tremble(n, sr, r, f)`: a smooth random curve, for modulations under 10 Hz, which the biquads clamp), thousands of short bubbles (`composer().perles`: each a resonance of 6–14 cycles, so a few milliseconds, rising a little, mostly soft, denser when the water breaks), a few low gurgles, a soft crackle; 14 s, two channels. `boucle/riviere` (the gorge) the same way, darker. Spectral centroid 2.6 → 1.3 kHz (`sons/eau_test.mjs`, `sons/spectro.py` in the session scratchpad: spectrograms before/after; an MP3 was sent to the owner). Level 0.008 → 0.007, heard within 16 m instead of 20. Making them costs about +0.25 s in the worker (Gokayama only).
  - **The bridge.** Its stream walls were left out within 1.9 m (their 1 m round ends covered the deck), so from the bank beside the bridge one stepped down into the bed and could not climb out (the slope rule). They are back, limited in height (`monde.mur(…, bas, haut)`, `haut` = `PONT.y` + 0.05): they stop whoever is on the banks, not whoever is on the deck (`gk_marche.py` routes `pont-*`: across both ways, stopped on the bank beside it).
  - **Flickers.** The fire shed's front corner posts had their faces in the planes of the side walls: now 4 cm proud (and the bucket shelf 1 cm off the base). The attic sign « 養蚕 »: its posts' front faces were in the plane of the painted text; now behind the board.
  - **The inn.** The « 民宿 庄川荘 » board went through the okami: moved 1.4 m along the facade; she stands beside the door.
  - **The paper workshop, rebuilt** (« refais le stand de la dame au papier washi »; it was a box vat, a lid and bare boards): six posts, a shingle roof, the back wall of standing planks (toward the stream) and a low west wall with a shelf of paper rolls; a plank vat (`CUVE`) with corner posts and milky water; the bamboo screen frame (sukigeta) hanging from two springy bamboo poles, rocked over the water by the grandmother (the frame is an `animes` object; her new pose `washi` in `personnages.js` uses the same sine); the stack of wet sheets under a lever press with a hanging stone; the beating board, its pulp and two mallets; buckets; the « 和紙 » noren and a paper lantern lit at night; a table of finished sheets (white and dyed); outside, four ginkgo drying boards leaning on a pole with a sheet on each face (one pink), and kōzo bark folded over a pole, by tied bunches. For the stamp, the traveller now stands at (0.5, 0.3) beside her. About 3,200 triangles (was 470).
  - **Aprons** (every passer-by with `tablier`): a flat board floated in front of the chest, like a sheet held in the hands; now a curved panel around the front from the waist to the knees (wider over a skirt) and its tie.
  - **Off the paths** (« les buissons, la lanterne et le champ dépassent sur le chemin »): five lanterns (each now 1.8 m from its path's line), three hedges (shortened or moved), the garden by the shrine path (it lay across it) and a water-cannon hut that stood on the main path. `chemins_obstacles.mjs` (session scratchpad `gokayama/`) lists what lies within a path's painted width (1.15 m + 0.2 of noise) plus its own radius; `pousser.mjs x z d` gives the nearest point at distance d from every path.
  - **The secret replaced** by « Les lucioles des Heike » (see Secret above), the owner's choice among four proposals (a scarecrow playing « 1, 2, 3, soleil », a kappa in the stream, the builders' carved marks were the others). The old one is gone: the separate board, the pit, the chest, `banniere()`, `billet()`, the sounds `creux` and `boite` for this place.
  - **Not reproduced:** « je peux pas avancer… à cause des dalles en pierre devant l'hôtel » (with a screenshot of the shrine's slab path: probably « l'autel », heard by the dictation as « l'hôtel »). Keyboard and phone-joystick walks (`gk_joystick.py`, a 390 × 844 touch context at CPU ÷4) up the shrine stairs, along the slabs to the offering box and into the minshuku all pass; asked the owner for the exact spot. Found the next round: the top of the shrine stairs (below).
  - Checks: `gk_test.mjs` (55 619 cells reachable, nothing out of reach; 732 k triangles, +0.1 %), `gk_partie.py` (the 5 stamps, the mino, the secret, night and morning), `gk_dedans.py`, `gk_grenier.py`, `copl_mm.mjs gokayama 20` (the workshop: only bottom faces on the floor and the bamboo's own joints) and `22` (the shed), `vues_gk.py` before/after (`--port 8124`) and at night.
- **Gokayama, the owner's third round (2026-10-24, late; four screenshots).**
  - **The shrine stairs, rebuilt** (« refais l'escalier du sanctuaire parce que là je ne peux pas encore avancer »: the slabs « devant l'hôtel » of the round before). The cause: one walked a straight ramp over five 30 cm steps; at its top the natural ground, which rose to the esplanade with a smoothstep, passed 1 cm above the ramp over its last 10 cm, so the walker stood on that ground (`poserPied` takes the higher), whose slope (over 0.85) stopped him: he got through only by sliding sideways, slowly, or not at all (the earlier walking tests slid through). Now `ESC_S` (module level): eight solid steps with 36 cm treads (2.9 m long, the foot at `SANCTUAIRE.marches[1]`), walked along `E.chemin` through each tread's middle (`monde.chemin`), the ground under them kept at the foot's level (`solSansEscalier`), sloped side walls (sode-ishi: sheared boxes with a wider coping), a square post at each end; the esplanade's front wall got a coping 8 cm above the gravel (flush, its top lay in the ground's plane). `gk_escalier.py [--tel] [--course] [--cpu 4] [--de x,z] [--cap degrees]` (session scratchpad `gokayama/`) pushes straight ahead without touching the camera and logs the position every 0.1 s: before, at the top the speed fell from 2.4 to 0.3 m/s for a second, sliding sideways; now full speed up and down. `esc_haut.mjs z` steps along the stairs in Node and says what stops.
  - **In the engine:** `poserPied` (`monde.js`) lets a surface within 2 cm under the natural ground win over it. `sol_dessus.mjs <place>` (session scratchpad `balade/`) lists, every 5 cm on every surface and outside walls and circles, where the natural ground is above the surface by less than a step and steeper than the place's slope limit: Gokayama had 2 more points (1 cm, at the side of the belvedere stairs' top), now none; Aogashima none; Itsukushima has no slope limit (`pente` null), so its pagoda stairs' points stop no one. `regions.mjs`: Itsukushima and Aogashima reach exactly the same cells as before.
  - **A second bridge** (« rajoute un deuxième pont à l'endroit du screen »: the stream west of the paddy, between house g2 and the shrine): `CHEMIN_PONT`, a path from g2's path over the stream to the torii, and `PONTS`, a bridge wherever the main path or it crosses the stream (the same builder; near each, the stream's walls stop only those off its deck).
  - **The minshuku's veranda** (« on peut passer à travers le côté de la maison »): its deck was only drawn; one walked at ground level, the legs in the boards, up to an invisible wall in its middle. Now a surface (one 50 cm step), a flat stepping stone in its middle (a second surface), the bench line at its edge.
  - **The museum's display shelf** flickered: its uprights had the boards' depth, so their front and back faces lay in the boards' planes. Now 2 cm deeper on each side, and the boards end inside them.
  - **The golden firefly, easier to find** (« je ne trouve pas la luciole la nuit… plus visible »): it waited 60 m from the okami, by the shrine; now on the north bank 10 m from her, then eleven hops along the north bank to the second bridge and on as before. The first time one comes within 12 m the camera shows it over the traveller's shoulder (`lucioleLoin`); within 3.6 m, `lucioleDoree` and the companion's line. Bigger (halo 0.8, heart 0.16, × the field-of-view correction), a spark of constant screen size (`phare`, 7–9 px), a trail of sparks while flying (`trainee`, 16 points fading in 0.9 s), a stronger, wider gold light (r 2.4). The schoolboy's hint now says « près du minshuku ».
  - Checks: `gk_test.mjs` (55 623 cells, nothing out of reach; 734 k triangles), `gk_marche.py` (new routes `veranda*`, `pont2-*`, and the shrine's), `copl_mm.mjs gokayama 17` (the stairs), `4` (the shelf) and `15` (the bridges): no overlap left there; `gk_heike.py` (computer, and `--tel --compagnon pon`), `gk_partie.py`, `gk_dedans.py`, `gk_grenier.py`, `partie.py`, `nuit.py`, `marche_escaliers.py`.
  - **The lit windows, softer** (the same night; « la lueur des fenêtres scintille un peu trop, il faut que ce soit plus doux »). From 25 m on, the shōji's crossbars are about a pixel wide: dark against the lit paper, they shimmered as the camera moved, and the night glow spread it. The crossbars now have their own colour (`P.shojiCadre`, 1 unit from `boisNoir`, same wood texture) and kind (`treillis`, 14 in `SORTES`, through `sortesNuit`): at night they take a warm light that grows from 7 to 28 m away (`vLueur` 4 in the decor shader), so a far window reads as a lit paper pane and a near one keeps its dark lattice; the glow is softer (see Glow above). `fenetres_scintille.py <name>` (session scratchpad `gokayama/`) slides the camera 2 cm per frame in front of a gable at 24 and 38 m and measures how much the windows' pixels jump beyond the steady drift: 28.3 → 12.3 ‰ and 21.3 → 10.8 ‰. GPU time unchanged (`gpu_charge.py --nuit`: Gokayama 2.3–2.9 ms, Itsukushima 1.4 ms, before and after).
- **Himeji Castle (2026-10-25; the owner: « C'est quel lieu le suivant ? Moi j'avais pensé au château de Himeji », then picked, from my proposals, Okiku's plate as the secret and the white heron headdress as the reward).** `himeji.js`, map id `himeji-castle` (added to `BALADES`), TikTok `7649489240199531778`. The « White Heron » (白鷺城), its keep of 1609 never destroyed: the inner moat and the wooden bridge, the great gate (大手門) in the white earthen wall, the Sannomaru lawn and its cherry trees, then the maze of three terraces with stone walls, gates and white walls pierced with loopholes, the courtyard of Okiku's well, and the great keep (five white tiers, grey roofs with white plaster edges) with a small keep and its corridor; the Nishi-no-maru and its long corridor to the west (not walked). Around: wooded slopes, then the town (`immeuble`: boxes and window strips, lit at night through `sortesNuit` `vitreVille`), the avenue to the station, mountains far away. Distances are tightened. The keep is reachable inside: its basement hall (the two great pillars, gun and spear racks, paper lamps) and, by « Monter » (a cut, like Gokayama's ladder: `jeu.placer` to `Y_HAUT`), the top floor (open windows on the town, the little Osakabe shrine and its plaque).
  - **The plan** (module constants): `DOUVES` (16 m wide, the water at −2, `eauxCalmes` with `lieu.eauCalme` { boue, bord, clarte }: green, the walls' grey reflection, clear enough to see the koi), `PONT`, `ENCEINTE_Z`, `OTEMON`, `ESPLANADE`, the terraces `T1` (3 m), `T2` (6), `T3` (9.5), `NISHI` (5), `ESCALIERS` (solid steps between two stone murets, walked along `monde.chemin`; the ground under them at the foot's level; each foot on its lower terrace), `HISHI` (the Diamond Gate: gold diamonds on its beam, bell-shaped windows rimmed with gold), `PORTES` (kōraimon gates in walls across the terraces: i, ro, ha; ni at the top of the last stairs, inside a little square court, masugata), `IMPASSE` (the dead end where the plate lies), `PUITS` and `COUR`, `DONJON` (socle 22 × 18 × 5 m), `ETAGES` (each tier on the roof below it), `SALLE`, `HAUT`, `PETIT`, `MARCHAND` (Bunbuku under the cherry trees, west of the lawn), `DEPART` (on the bridge: the gate ahead, the keep above it, the « hero » view), `ARRIVEE` (the sidewalk at the bridge's foot, where the bus stops: the 大手門前 bus-stop sign and timetable, the « 特別史跡 姫路城 » stele, white guard rails along the street and the moat).
  - **Stone walls** (`bande`, `rempart`, `muret`): rows of stones of uneven widths (45–85 cm), joints staggered from one row to the next, four greys drawn stone by stone (`TONS`, all `M.pierre`); the walls' fan profile (`EVENTAIL`, ōgi-no-kōbai: nearly vertical at the top, flaring at the foot). The top edge sits `e0` (35 cm) in front of the terrace's edge, a row of flat coping stones between: the terrain falls in a straight line over one metre from the edge (the 1 m grid draws that chord), and a concave wall started at the edge would lie behind it (checked numerically: the wall stays ≥ 6 cm in front). The first version was a checkerboard of three greys (« like tiles »).
  - **White earthen walls** (`dobei`, `enceinte`: pieces ≤ 10 m): stone base, plaster, a two-slope tile cap and its white ridge, loopholes (round, triangular, square) on the side facing out (`dehors`). They stop the walker (`monde.mur`) and the camera (`monde.bloc`: it stops in front instead of filming the wall from behind). A piece overhangs only at the free ends of its wall (`debut`, `fin`), and a wall along z sits 12 mm higher than one along x (base, roof, ridge), so pieces and crossing walls never share a plane (`coplanaires_gros.mjs`: the overlaps went from 244 m² to hidden faces only). Every edge where one stops has a wall, a rail or a terrace's stone wall: `hm_bords.mjs` (session scratchpad `himeji/`) lists the edges of what one reaches where only the frame stops (none left; first run: the dead end's end, the lawn's corners, the bridge's foot).
  - **Buildings under roofs**: walls go up into the roof (`sousToit`: the height of a `toitJapon`'s underside above the walls), or one saw into the roof between the wall top and the eaves (the top floor's orange floor showed through); two-slope corridors get a plaster gable under their roof. Roofs: grey tiles with white plaster undersides and edges (`TUILES`), corners gently upturned; on the keep, triangular gables (`hafu`) alternating sides by tier, a curved one over the entrance (`karahafu`: an extruded cusped arch, its white gable and gold ornament), the top roof's irimoya gable and two shachihoko. The top floor's floor sits above the roof below (it was covered by its cap).
  - **Stamps**: `koi` (a bag of food from the old lady's stall, then feed the koi from the bridge: they rush, gulp at the surface, rings on the water; the koi are ×1.9, as the other animals are bigger than life), `hishi` (look up at the Diamond Gate), `sakura` (the picnic under the cherry trees: sit on the blue tarp, dango), `okiku` (the well's legend, by day), `tenshu` (the view from the top floor over the castle and the town).
  - **Reward: the white heron headdress** (`heron`, place `tete`, `tenues.js`; `FORMES.heron` in `personnages.js`): a little white egret perched on the head like a hat, its folded wings, the S-neck, the yellow bill, the nape plumes and three long aigrettes falling behind; the boy's spiky hair hidden under it.
  - **Secret: Okiku's tenth plate** (`SECRETS.himeji = 'okiku'`, the Banshū Sarayashiki ghost story). At night (the castle keeper offers the evening illumination), a blue fireball (hitodama: halo, heart, a pixel spark and a 14-point tail) floats over the well; « Écouter »: she counts the plates « ichi-mai… kyū-mai » (a porcelain clink each, `assiette`), sobs that one is missing (`voirIndice('okiku-nuit')`), the companion's line. From then on the plate shows in the corner of the dead end (a faint blue glow at night); « Ramasser » (into the bag), back to the well at night: « Rendre »: she counts to ten (`compteDix`), turns golden, rises among sparks and fades; a koban on the well's cover (`prendre`). By day the well tells its legend; with the plate, it says to come back at night; afterwards one prays there. Hints: the old lady (after the koi stamp), the guide in the keep at night, the schoolboy at night.
  - **Night**: the keeper brings the night and the morning back. The white plaster is `illumine` (floodlit), the paper lamps and shōji glow, the town's windows light up; night lines for everyone (the stall and the picnic closed).
  - **Animals** (`betes.js`): white egrets on the lawns (`aigrettes`: the grey heron's builder with a white robe, `echassier(R)`; they fly off without a cry), red dragonflies; the koi in the moat (the place's own `animes`).
  - **Sound and music**: sounds `assiette` (a porcelain plate set on another: two clinks) and `plouf` (food scattered on the water, a gulp), plus `cloche`, `claque`, `papier`; birds (black kite, uguisu), insects at night. Its tune (`AIRS.himeji`): A minor, a melody on the in scale (mi fa la si do, that of « Sakura sakura »), the festival flute (fue), koto, taiko, 88 BPM; at night the music box at 66. Stamp motifs (`carnet.js`): a cherry blossom, the Diamond Gate, the well and its flame, the keep, a koi.
  - **Bunbuku** under the cherry trees (`MARCHAND` −39.5, 1.5); **fireworks** behind the keep (`feux` z −125).
  - **Measured** (2026-10-25): 233 k triangles of decor; phone at CPU ÷4 (`gk_fluide.py himeji`): 5.6 ms median, p95 5.7–11.1 ms, no frame over 25 ms (Gokayama the same); 153–247 draw calls and 0.84–0.94 M triangles per frame at the set views (`gk_appels.py himeji`; Itsukushima 0.47–0.50 M, Gokayama 1.27–1.47 M); GPU (`gpu_charge.py --apres`): 1.6–1.7 ms (Itsukushima and Aogashima 1.5, Gokayama 2.3).
  - Checks (session scratchpad `himeji/`): `node ../gokayama/gk_test.mjs himeji` (builds, everything in reach), `hm_bords.mjs`, `hm_vues.py [prefix] [--nuit] [--tel] [--seul a,b]`, `hm_partie.py [--tel] [--garcon] [--compagnon c] [--secret]` (the 5 stamps, the headdress, the secret, the morning), `hm_marche.py` (13 routes held with W: the bridge, the three stairs both ways, every gate, into the keep), `hm_coiffe.py` (the headdress on both travellers), `vues_libres.py … --lieu himeji`, `charge_tous.py` (the four places load with no error), `coplanaires_gros.mjs himeji` and `copl_mm.mjs himeji <object>` (session scratchpad `balade/`).
  - **The owner's first round (2026-10-25, 18 screenshots).**
    - **Roofs in an X** (the Nishi corridor) and **roof pieces inside the top floor**: `deuxPans` tilted each slab, then turned it with the roof, in three.js's default Euler order `XYZ`, which turns first: a roof turned a quarter turn leaned along its ridge. Its slabs now pass `ordre: 'YXZ'` (tilt, then turn); this also fixed every keep gable but the west ones (the east ones were an upside-down V, the north and south ones an X). The round and triangular loopholes of north–south walls had the same fault (edge-on). Tier 3's gables were 2.4 m deep and came through the top floor's floor: their depth now stops at its wall (`prof`).
    - **A slit down the keep's corners**: at each corner a rampart flank stopped 3 cm (`o`) short of its neighbour's plane, and the light showed through. `rempart` extends each flank, and its coping, by `e + o`.
    - **Can't walk round the keep, on the left**: rectangular buildings were thick wall segments (`monde.mur`, a capsule): their rounded ends stuck out by their half-width. The small keep's reached the terrace wall, closing the way west of it; each corner tower had an invisible wall about 2 m in front of it; the Diamond Gate's narrowed the passage to 1.8 m. They are now `plein` surfaces of their footprint (+25 cm). One can walk right round both keeps (`hm_marche.py` route `tour-donjon`).
    - **Trees through roofs and walls**: five cherries and the corner tower's pine moved; the hill's trees, placed at random, shrink by 12 % steps (or are left out) while their crown would reach a white wall or building, which `dobei`, `yagura` and the Nishi corridor note in `BATIS` (`gene`), without changing the random draws of the other trees. Check: `arbres_murs.mjs` (session scratchpad `himeji/`, every foliage clump against every plaster and tile piece): 8 → 0.
    - **Grass at the foot of every tree**: on the terraces, a round of grass in the ground map (`ARBRES_TERRASSES`: the pines, now `PINS` at module level, and the terrace cherries) and nine 3D tufts (`herbesAuPied`). Grass tufts no longer grow on the ground just outside the terraces' edges, behind the stone faces (they poked through the stones and the coping; `aspectSol` gives that strip dirt), nor at the foot of the stone faces on the lawn (a gravel band).
    - **Gaps between gates and walls**: the great gate now stands on the wall line (`OTEMON.z`, it was a metre behind it) and the walls go to its posts; every kōraimon's walls end at its post's outer face, without overhang (`enceinte(…, { libre0, libre1 })`); beside the Diamond Gate, set back from the terrace edge, a short wall joins the edge wall to each wing. White walls that stopped at the foot of a higher terrace's flared stone face (the gates' walls, the well court's) now run into it (`z0 − 0.58`): the top of the stone leans back, and a gap opened there. The lawn's north walls sit 30 cm further back, against the first terrace's foot.
    - **The floating lantern** was the old lady's stall lantern, placed in world coordinates instead of the stall's frame (it hung at (0.7, 0.48), mid-lawn). The three lanterns standing in the paths at the foot of the Diamond Gate stairs went onto the lawn; the gate's panel stands against the wall's foot.
    - **The keep's door flickered**: inside the entrance passage, the hall's south wall pieces and the passage's side walls had their end faces in one plane (`scintille.py`, session scratchpad: the camera slides 1 cm a frame and a heat map shows each pixel's jumps; 57,000 → 1,600 flickering pixels). The lintel and the passage floor were also flush with the side walls.
    - **Inside the keep**: the steep stairs moved off a post that stood in their middle (`MONTEE.x`); posts along the walls and their beam (nageshi); guns lying on pegs, three per bay, and long spears on pegs in the posts; two samurai armours on their chests (look at them: `armure`); two nagamochi chests, rice bales, water barrels and fire buckets, the stone sink. Top floor: dark posts and beams; the Osakabe shrine rebuilt (a cypress shrine with a copper nagare roof, chigi and katsuogi, gilded doors, the shimenawa, the mirror, offerings, the saisen box, the bell and its braided rope above it).
    - **The engine: indoor decor** (`balade.js`): an object of `lieu.objets` with `dedans: [x0, x1, z0, z1]` is drawn only while the camera is in that rectangle. The keep's new interior (`ad`) uses it (the keep's footprint + 9 m): from the rest of the castle, it costs nothing.
    - **Less empty lawn**: trimmed azaleas (ō-karikomi, sort `buisson`) along the lawn's walls; where the paths ended against a bare wall, a wisteria trellis with two benches (east; its leaves and flowers fall in winter), a large stone carved « 白鷺 » between two small pines (north-east), a tsukubai with its bamboo spout (west), two more lanterns.
    - **The town's buildings** (`immeuble`): offices (window rows between pillars, a lit shop window and its sign band, sometimes a vertical sign), apartment blocks (a balcony per floor) and, among the tallest, glass towers (a curtain wall, floor lines, mullions); a parapet, the lift room, sometimes a water tank and air conditioners on the roof. Only the faces turned toward the castle get windows and details. Unlit windows are nearly the colour of the lit ones (`P.vitreVille`): by day they made light and dark stripes.
    - The top-floor view (stamp `tenshu`) places the traveller behind the camera (their hat showed).
    - **Measured**: 262 k triangles of decor (+12 %); per frame +4 % from the lawn, the bridge and the keep's foot (+6 % on the top floor), draw calls +3 to +7; GPU (`gpu_charge.py`, alternating): 1.60–1.75 ms against 1.57–1.88 before; phone at CPU ÷4 (`gk_fluide.py himeji`): medians 5.6 ms, the same as before, no frame over 25 ms (the p95 swings between 5.7 and 11 ms in both versions). Checks: `gk_test.mjs himeji` (nothing out of reach), `hm_bords.mjs` (no invisible wall), `hm_marche.py` (16 routes), `hm_partie.py` (computer, and phone with the boy and Pon: the 5 stamps, the headdress, the secret, the morning), `charge_tous.py`, `coplanaires_gros.mjs` and `copl_mm.mjs` (what is left is hidden, the same colour, or the toitJapon roofs' hips), night and the four seasons.
  - **The owner's second round (2026-10-25, five screenshots and a dictated list).**
    - **Two gaps in the walls** (and three more found the same way): a white wall that ended in front of a higher terrace's flared stone corner left, at the top, a gap through which the hill showed (the stone leans back as it rises): T1's north wall at T2's south-east corner (the owner's daytime screenshot), the lawn's west wall at the Nishi-no-maru's south-east corner (the night one, with an egret), and T2's north walls at T3's two corners. Each now turns into the stone (an `enceinte` point inside the face, `libre0`/`libre1` false), or, at the lawn's north-west, runs into it. Rule: a wall end must be inside the stone at every height (the face's position at height y is `bord` + `EVENTAIL`), never just touch its foot.
    - **Roof edges** (`toitH`, every `toitJapon` of the place): four white hip ridges (sumi-mune: a `tube` along the roof's own corner curve, no outline) with a grey onigawara at each tip, and the eave band grey (`TUILES.avant`).
    - **The Diamond Gate, rebuilt** (its own object, culled when out of view): front kagami-bashira and thinner back posts on stone plinths, iron feet with a gold band; the kabuki beam with two gilt hanabishi on black lacquer plates and gilt fittings, the back beam; open plank doors with three iron bands and gold studs, a kuguri door; the wings' end posts and tie beams; under the turret, a row of joist ends, a dark base band and a plaster nageshi; two bell windows (`cloche`, `katomado`: a gold ring, a black lacquer frame, shōji paper and lacquered bars, lit at night) and black-lattice windows over paper on all four sides; an irimoya roof (the white gable, `gegyo`, onigawara). Its stamp text still fits.
    - **The great keep**: each tier's windows framed (`fenetres(…, { cadre: true })`: a plaster surround in relief, a sill, six bars) under a plaster band (nageshi); more gables, as on the real keep's stacked roofs: twin small ones east and west of the first and third roofs, a big one south and north of the second, small ones south and north of the fourth (stopped at the top floor's wall, their ridge under its windows); every gable and the kara-hafu got a gilt `gegyo` (a heart between two curls) instead of a gold square.
    - **The tea stall** (茶屋, `CHAYA`, on the Bizen-maru east of the keep, against the wall: « un stand de nourriture sur le côté droit du grand donjon »): a wooden stall under a tile roof, its counter, the steaming pot of Himeji oden (eaten with ginger soy sauce: a local speciality), dango, tea, three indigo noren (おでん, 茶屋, だんご), a matcha soft-serve sign, a menu board (お品書き), bins; in front two red-felt benches (sittable, `bancs`) under a red parasol. The keeper (`chaya`: a taste of oden and the companion's line, `odenCompagnon`; closed at night) and a visitor eating dango (`gourmande`, seated). Texts in the three languages.
    - **Clouds** in the sky (cumulus of flattened `IcosahedronGeometry` lobes, 600–900 m away, no outline).
    - **The walk's menu on phones** (`balade.css`, `balade.js`): it overflowed the screen and could neither scroll nor close. It now scrolls inside its box (`max-height: calc(100dvh - 24px)`, `overflow-y: auto`, `touch-action: pan-y`) under a sticky header « Menu » with a red ×. A sticky element in a scroll container counts `top` from the container's padding edge (14 px here), hence `top: -9px`.
